'./types';\n\ninterface Particle {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  vx: number;\n  vy: number;\n  baseRadius: number;\n  color: string;\n  alpha: number;\n  angle: number;\n  orbitRadius: number;\n  orbitSpeed: number;\n}\n\ninterface BurstWave {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  radius: number;\n  maxRadius: number;\n  alpha: number;\n  color: string;\n}\n\nexport default function ParticleCanvas({\n  settings,\n  onFpsUpdate,\n}: ParticleCanvasProps) {\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n\n  const pointerRef = useRef<CanvasPointer>({\n    x: 0,\n    y: 0,\n    isDown: false,\n    isActive: false,\n  });\n\n  const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n  const burstsRef = useRef<BurstWave[]>([]);\n  const animFrameIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  const lastTimeRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n  const frameCountRef = useRef<number>(0);\n  const fpsTimerRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n  const autoFloatTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);\n\n  // 指定色セットからランダム色を取得\n  const getRandomColor = useCallback((colors: string[]): string => {\n    return colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];\n  }, []);\n\n  // パーティクル生成/補充\n  const initParticles = useCallback(\n    (width: number, height: number, targetCount: number) => {\n      const colors = COLOR_THEMES[settings.colorTheme].colors;\n      const currentParticles = particlesRef.current;\n\n      if (currentParticles.length < targetCount) {\n        const needed = targetCount - currentParticles.length;\n        for (let i = 0; i < needed; i++) {\n          const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n          const speed = (Math.random() * 1.5 + 0.5) * settings.speed;\n          currentParticles.push({\n            x: Math.random() * width,\n            y: Math.random() * height,\n            vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n            vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n            baseRadius: (Math.random() * 0.8 + 0.6) * settings.size,\n            color: getRandomColor(colors),\n            alpha: Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.5,\n            angle: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,\n            orbitRadius: Math.random() * 140 + 40,\n            orbitSpeed: (Math.random() * 0.02 + 0.01) * (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1),\n          });\n        }\n      } else if (currentParticles.length > targetCount) {\n        particlesRef.current = currentParticles.slice(0, targetCount);\n      }\n\n      // 既存のパーティクルの色やサイズを最新設定に更新\n      particlesRef.current.forEach((p) => {\n        p.baseRadius = (Math.random() * 0.8 + 0.6) * settings.size;\n        if (!colors.includes(p.color)) {\n          p.color = getRandomColor(colors);\n        }\n      });\n    },\n    [settings.colorTheme, settings.size, settings.speed, getRandomColor]\n  );\n\n  // 衝撃波（バースト）を発生させる関数\n  const triggerBurstAt = useCallback(\n    (x: number, y: number) => {\n      const themeColors = COLOR_THEMES[settings.colorTheme].colors;\n      const burstColor = themeColors[0] || '#38bdf8';\n\n      burstsRef.current.push({\n        x,\n        y,\n        radius: 5,\n        maxRadius: Math.min(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight) * 0.35,\n        alpha: 0.9,\n        color: burstColor,\n      });\n\n      // 周囲のパーティクルを勢いよく吹き飛ばす\n      particlesRef.current.forEach((p) => {\n        const dx = p.x - x;\n        const dy = p.y - y;\n        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n        if (dist < 280) {\n          const power = ((280 - dist) / 280) * 18 * settings.speed;\n          p.vx += (dx / dist) * power;\n          p.vy += (dy / dist) * power;\n        }\n      });\n    },\n    [settings.colorTheme, settings.speed]\n  );\n\n  // 外部からのバーストイベント受診用\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleCustomBurst = () => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (!canvas) return;\n      const cx = pointerRef.current.isActive ? pointerRef.current.x : canvas.width / 2;\n      const cy = pointerRef.current.isActive ? pointerRef.current.y : canvas.height / 2;\n      triggerBurstAt(cx, cy);\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('trigger-particle-burst', handleCustomBurst);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('trigger-particle-burst', handleCustomBurst);\n    };\n  }, [triggerBurstAt]);\n\n  // Canvasリサイズ処理\n  const handleResize = useCallback(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!canvas || !container) return;\n\n    const width = container.clientWidth;\n    const height = container.clientHeight;\n    const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);\n\n    canvas.width = width * dpr;\n    canvas.height = height * dpr;\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (ctx) {\n      ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);\n    }\n\n    initParticles(width, height, settings.count);\n  }, [settings.count, initParticles]);\n\n  // 設定更新に応じたパーティクル数・スタイルの追従\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (container) {\n      initParticles(container.clientWidth, container.clientHeight, settings.count);\n    }\n  }, [settings.count, settings.colorTheme, settings.size, settings.speed, initParticles]);\n\n  // メインアニメーションループ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!canvas || !container) return;\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    let isRunning = true;\n\n    const render = (time: number) => {\n      if (!isRunning) return;\n\n      const delta = (time - lastTimeRef.current) / 1000;\n      lastTimeRef.current = time;\n\n      // FPS計測\n      frameCountRef.current += 1;\n      if (time - fpsTimerRef.current >= 500) {\n        const currentFps = Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / (time - fpsTimerRef.current));\n        onFpsUpdate(Math.min(currentFps, 120));\n        frameCountRef.current = 0;\n // トレイル描画（半透明黒で重ね塗り）\n      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';\n      ctx.fillStyle = currentTheme.background;\n      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0.04, Math.min(0.8, settings.trailLength));\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      // 加算合成で光彩感を演出\n      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n\n      // ポインタ座標の決定（非操作時はリサージュ曲線で滑らかに自動遊泳）\n      let const t = autoFloatTimeRef.current;\n        targetX = width / 2 + Math.sin(t * 1.3) * (width * 0.35);\n        targetY = height / 2 + Math.cos(t * 0.9) * (height * 0.28);\n      }\n\n      const particles = particlesRef.current;\n      const pCount = particles.length;\n\n      // 物理計算 & 更新\n      for (let i = 0; i < pCount; i++) {\n        const p = particles[i];\n        const dx = targetX - p.x;\n        const dy = targetY - p.y;\n        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n\n        // モード別挙動\n        if (settings.behaviorMode === 'follow') {\n          // 吸い寄せ\n          const force = Math.min(dist * 0.00045 * settings.speed, 0.45);\n          p.vx += (dx / dist) * force;\n          p.vy += (dy / dist) * force;\n        } else if (settings.behaviorMode === 'repel') {\n          // 反発\n          if (dist < 260) {\n            const force = ((260 - dist) / 260) * 0.75 * settings.speed;\n            p.vx -= (dx / dist) * force;\n            p.vy -= (dy / dist) * force;\n          }\n        } else if (settings.behaviorMode === 'orbit') {\n          // 周回軌道\n          p.angle += p.orbitSpeed * settings.speed;\n          const targetOrbitX = targetX + Math.cos(p.angle) * p.orbitRadius;\n          const targetOrbitY = targetY + Math.sin(p.angle) * p.orbitRadius;\n          p.vx += (targetOrbitX - p.x) * 0.02 * settings.speed;\n          p.vy += (targetOrbitY - p.y) * 0.02 * settings.speed;\n        } else if (settings.behaviorMode === 'stream') {\n          // 流星群（斜め方向の気流＋ポインタ付近での加速）\n          p.vx += 0.08 * settings.speed;\n          p.vy += 0.12 * settings.speed;\n          if (dist < 200) {\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * 0.3 * settings.speed;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * 0.3 * settings.speed;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // クリック中は引力を強化\n        if (pointerRef.current.isDown) {\n          const pressForce = Math.min(dist * 0.001 * settings.speed, 0.8);\n          p.vx += (dx / dist) * pressForce;\n          p.vy += (dy / dist) * pressForce;\n        }\n\n        // 減速摩擦\n        p.vx *= 0.94;\n        p.vy *= 0.94;\n\n        // 速度上限\n        const currentSpeed = Math.hypot(p.vx, p.vy);\n        const maxSpd = 16 * settings.speed;\n        if (currentSpeed > maxSpd) {\n          p.vx = (p.vx / currentSpeed) * maxSpd;\n          p.vy = (p.vy / currentSpeed) * maxSpd;\n        }\n\n        // 座標更新\n        p.x += p.vx;\n        p.y += p.vy;\n\n        // 画面端のラップアラウンド（飛び出し防止）\n        if (p.x < -30) p.x = width + 30;\n        if (p.x > width + 30) p.x = -30;\n        if (p.y < -30) p.y = height + 30;\n        if (p.y > height + 30) p.y = -30;\n\n        // パーティクル本体描画\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.baseRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      // 接続線描画 (オプション)\n      if (settings.connectLines) {\n        const maxDist = settings.connectionDistance;\n        ctx.lineWidth = 0.75;\n\n        for (let i = 0; i < pCount; i++) {\n          const p1 = particles[i];\n          // 全探索は負荷が高いため近隣のインデックスをステップ比較\n          for (let j = i + 1; j < Math.min(i + 28, pCount); j++) {\n            const p2 = particles[j];\n            const dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n            const dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n            const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n\n            if (dist < maxDist) {\n              const lineAlpha = (1 - dist / maxDist) * 0.38;\n              ctx.strokeStyle = p1.color;\n              ctx.globalAlpha = lineAlpha;\n              ctx.beginPath();\n              ctx.moveTo(p1.x, p1.y);\n              ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);\n              ctx.stroke();\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // バースト衝撃波の描画\n      const bursts = burstsRef.current;\n      for (let i = bursts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n        const b = bursts[i];\n        b.radius += (b.maxRadius - b.radius) * 0.12 + 2;\n        b.alpha *= 0.92;\n\n        ctx.save();\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.strokeStyle = b.color;\n        ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, (1 - b.radius / b.maxRadius) * 6);\n        ctx.globalAlpha = b.alpha;\n        ctx.stroke();\n        ctx.restore();\n\n        if (b.alpha < 0.03 || b.radius >= b.maxRadius * 0.98) {\n          bursts.splice(i, 1);\n        }\n      }\n\n      // ポインタ中心の微細な発光体\n      if (pointerRef.current.isActive) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(pointerRef.current.x, pointerRef.current.y, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.6;\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n    };\n\n    animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n\n    return () => {\n      isRunning = false;\n      if (animFrameIdRef.current !== null) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameIdRef.current);\n      }\n    };\n  }, [settings, onFpsUpdate]);\n\n  // ウィンドウリサイズ監視\n  useEffect(() => {\n    handleResize();\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    };\n  }, [handleResize]);\n\n  // ポインターイベント群\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    const rect = pointerRef.current.x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    pointerRef.current.y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n    pointerRef.current.isActive = true;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.isDown = false;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.isDown = false;\n    pointerRef.current.isActive = false;\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div\n useRef<number>(0);\n    const fpsTimerRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n    const autoFloatTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);\n\n    // 指定色セットからランダム色を取得\n    const getRandomColor = useCallback((colors: string[]): string => {\n      return colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];\n    }, []);\n\n    // パーティクル生成/補充\n    const initParticles = useCallback(\n      (width: canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (ctx) {\n        ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);\n      }\n\n      initParticles(width, height, settings.count);\n    }, [settings.count,   let isRunning = true;\n\n      const render = (time: number) => {\n        if (!isRunning) return;\n\n        const delta = (time - lastTimeRef.current) / 1000;\n        lastTimeRef.current = time;\n\n        // FPS計測\n        frameCountRef.current += 1;\n        if (time - fpsTimerRef.current >= 500) {\n          const currentFps = Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / (time - fpsTimerRef.current));\n          onFpsUpdate(Math.min(currentFps, 120));\n          frameCountRef.current = 0;\n          fpsTimerRef.current = time;\n        }\n\n        const width = targetY - p.y;\n          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n\n          // モード別挙動\n          if (settings.behaviorMode === 'follow') {\n            // 吸い寄せ\n            const force = Math.min(dist * 0.00045 * settings.speed, 0.45);\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * force;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * force;\n          } else if (settings.behaviorMode === 'repel') {\n            // 反発\n            if (dist < 260) {\n              const force = ((260 - dist) / 260) * 0.75 * settings.speed;\n              p.vx += (dx / dist) * force;\n              p.vy += (dy / dist) * force;\n            }\n          } else if (settings.behaviorMode === 'orbit') {\n            // 周回軌道\n            p.angle += p.orbitSpeed * settings.speed;\n            const targetOrbitX = targetX + += (targetOrbitY - p.y) * 0.02 * settings.speed;\n          } else if (settings.behaviorMode === 'stream') {\n            // 流星群（斜め方向の気流＋ポインタ付近での加速）\n            p.vx += 0.08 * settings.speed;\n            p.vy += 0.12 * settings.speed;\n            if (dist < 200) {\n              p.vx += (dx / dist) * 0.3 * settings.speed;\n              p.vy += (dy / dist) * 0.3 * settings.speed;\n            }\n          }\n\n          // クリック中は引力を強化\n          if (pointerRef.current.isDown) {\n            const pressForce = Math.min(dist * 0.001 * settings.speed, 0.8);\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * + 1; j < maxJ; j++) {\n              const p2 = particles[j];\n              const dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n              const dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n              const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n\n              if (dist < maxDist) {\n                const lineAlpha = (1 - dist / maxDist) * 0.38;\n                ctx.strokeStyle = p1.color;\n                ctx.globalAlpha = lineAlpha;\n バースト衝撃波の描画\n        const bursts = burstsRef.current;\n        for (let i = bursts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n          const b = bursts[i];\n          b.radius += (b.maxRadius - b.radius) * 0.12 + ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, (1 - b.radius / b.maxRadius) * 6);\n          ctx.globalAlpha = b.alpha;\n          ctx.stroke();\n          ctx.restore();\n\n          if (b.alpha < 0.03 || b.radius >= | 'stream';\n\nexport interface ParticleSettings {\n  count: number;\n  size: number;\n  speed: number;\n  trailLength: number;\n  connectLines: boolean;\n  connectionDistance: number;\n  colorTheme: ColorThemeType;\n  behaviorMode: BehaviorModeType;\n}\n\nexport interface ColorThemeInfo {\n  id: ColorThemeType;\n  name: string;\n  colors: string[];\n  background: string;\n}\n\nexport interface CanvasPointer {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  isDown: boolean;\n  isActive: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface ParticleCanvasHandle {\n  triggerBurst: (x?: number, y?: number) => void;\n}\n\nexport interface ParticleCanvasProps {\n  settings: ParticleSettings;\n  onFpsUpdate?: (fps: number) => void;\n}\n\nexport interface ControlsProps {\n  settings: ParticleSettings;\n  onSettingsChange: (newSettings: ParticleSettings) => void;\n  onBurst: () => void;\n  isCollapsed: boolean;\n  onToggleCollapse: () => void;\n}\n\nexport const COLOR_THEMES = {\n  aurora: {\n    id: 'aurora',\n    name: 'オーロラ',\n    colors: ['#38bdf8', '#34d399', '#818cf8', '#a7f3d0'],\n    background: '#060913',\n  },\n  neon: {\n    id: 'neon',\n    name: 'ネオン',\n    colors: ['#f43f5e', '#ec4899', '#06b6d4', '#facc15'],\n    background: '#090514',\n  },\n  sakura: {\n    id: 'sakura',\n    name: '桜吹雪',\n    colors: ['#fbcfe8', '#f472b6', '#fda4af', '#ffffff'],\n    background: '#130910',\n  },\n  cosmic: {\n    id: 'cosmic',\n    name: '宇宙',\n    colors: ['#c084fc', '#a855f7', '#60a5fa', '#38bdf8'],\n    background: '#040612',\n  },\n  fire: {\n    id: 'fire',\n    name: '紅蓮炎',\n    colors: ['#fb923c', '#ef4444', '#f59e0b', '#fde047'],\n    background: '#140705',\n  },\n} satisfies Record<ColorThemeType, ColorThemeInfo>;\n\nexport const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: ParticleSettings = {\n  count: 350,\n  size: 2.2,\n  speed: 1.0,\n  trailLength: 0.18,\n  connectLines: true,\n  connectionDistance: 95,\n  colorTheme: 'aurora',\n  behaviorMode: 'follow',\n};","language":"typescript"},{"path":"src/ParticleCanvas.tsx","content":"import {\n  useEffect,\n  useRef,\n  useCallback,\n  forwardRef,\n  useImperativeHandle,\n} from 'react';\nimport { ParticleCanvasProps, COLOR_THEMES, CanvasPointer } from './types';\n\nexport interface ParticleCanvasHandle {\n  triggerBurst: (x?: number, y?: number) => void;\n}\n\ninterface Particle {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  vx: number;\n  vy: number;\n  baseRadius: number;\n  color: string;\n  alpha: number;\n  angle: number;\n  orbitRadius: number;\n  orbitSpeed: number;\n}\n\ninterface BurstWave {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  radius: number;\n  maxRadius: number;\n  alpha: number;\n  color: string;\n}\n\nconst ParticleCanvas = forwardRef<ParticleCanvasHandle, ParticleCanvasProps>(\n  function ParticleCanvas({ settings, onFpsUpdate }, ref) {\n    const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n    const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n\n    const pointerRef = useRef<CanvasPointer>({\n      x: 0,\n      y: 0,\n      isDown: false,\n      isActive: false,\n    });\n\n    const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n    const burstsRef = useRef<BurstWave[]>([]);\n    const animFrameIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n    const lastTimeRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n    const frameCountRef = useRef<number>(0);\n    const fpsTimerRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n    const autoFloatTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);\n\n    // 指定色セットからランダム色を取得\n    const getRandomColor = useCallback((colors: string[]): string => {\n      return colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];\n    }, []);\n\n    // パーティクル生成/補充\n    const initParticles = useCallback(\n      (width: number, height: number, targetCount: number) => {\n        const colors = COLOR_THEMES[settings.colorTheme].colors;\n        const currentParticles = particlesRef.current;\n\n        if (currentParticles.length < targetCount) {\n          const needed = targetCount - currentParticles.length;\n          for (let i = 0; i < needed; i++) {\n            const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n            const speed = (Math.random() * 1.5 + 0.5) * settings.speed;\n            currentParticles.push({\n              x: Math.random() * width,\n              y: Math.random() * height,\n              vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n              vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n              baseRadius: (Math.random() * 0.8 + 0.6) * settings.size,\n              color: getRandomColor(colors),\n              alpha: Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.5,\n              angle: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,\n              orbitRadius: Math.random() * 140 + 40,\n              orbitSpeed: (Math.random() * 0.02 + 0.01) * (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1),\n            });\n          }\n        } else if (currentParticles.length > targetCount) {\n          particlesRef.current = currentParticles.slice(0, targetCount);\n        }\n\n        // 既存のパーティクルの色やサイズを最新設定に更新\n        particlesRef.current.forEach((p) => {\n          p.baseRadius = (Math.random() * 0.8 + 0.6) * settings.size;\n          if (!colors.includes(p.color)) {\n            p.color = getRandomColor(colors);\n          }\n        });\n      },\n      [settings.colorTheme, settings.size, settings.speed, getRandomColor]\n    );\n\n    // 衝撃波（バースト）を発生させる関数\n    const triggerBurstAt = useCallback(\n      (x: number, y: number) => {\n        const themeColors = COLOR_THEMES[settings.colorTheme].colors;\n        const burstColor = themeColors[0] || '#38bdf8';\n\n        burstsRef.current.push({\n          x,\n          y,\n          radius: 5,\n          maxRadius: Math.min(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight) * 0.35,\n          alpha: 0.9,\n          color: burstColor,\n        });\n\n        // 周囲のパーティクルを勢いよく吹き飛ばす\n        particlesRef.current.forEach((p) => {\n          const dx = p.x - x;\n          const dy = p.y - y;\n          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n          if (dist < 280) {\n            const power = ((280 - dist) / 280) * 18 * settings.speed;\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * power;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * power;\n          }\n        });\n      },\n      [settings.colorTheme, settings.speed]\n    );\n\n    // 親コンポーネントから直接呼び出せる命令的APIの提供\n    useImperativeHandle(\n      ref,\n      () => ({\n        triggerBurst: (customX?: number, customY?: number) => {\n          const container = containerRef.current;\n          const defaultX = pointerRef.current.isActive\n            ? pointerRef.current.x\n            : (container?.clientWidth ?? window.innerWidth) / 2;\n          const defaultY = pointerRef.current.isActive\n            ? pointerRef.current.y\n            : (container?.clientHeight ?? window.innerHeight) / 2;\n\n          triggerBurstAt(customX ?? defaultX, customY ?? defaultY);\n        },\n      }),\n      [triggerBurstAt]\n    );\n\n    // Canvasリサイズ処理（setTransformで累積スケーリングを防止）\n    const handleResize = useCallback(() => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      const container = containerRef.current;\n      if (!canvas || !container) return;\n\n      const width = container.clientWidth;\n      const height = container.clientHeight;\n      const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);\n\n      canvas.width = width * dpr;\n      canvas.height = height * dpr;\n\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (ctx) {\n        ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);\n      }\n\n      initParticles(width, height, settings.count);\n    }, [settings.count, initParticles]);\n\n    // 設定更新に応じたパーティクル数・スタイルの追従\n    useEffect(() => {\n      const container = containerRef.current;\n      if (container) {\n        initParticles(container.clientWidth, container.clientHeight, settings.count);\n      }\n    }, [settings.count, settings.colorTheme, settings.size, settings.speed, initParticles]);\n\n    // メインアニメーションループ\n    useEffect(() => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      const container = containerRef.current;\n      if (!canvas || !container) return;\n\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!ctx) return;\n\n      let isRunning = true;\n\n      const render = (time: number) => {\n        if (!isRunning) return;\n\n        const delta = (time - lastTimeRef.current) / 1000;\n        lastTimeRef.current = time;\n\n        // FPS計測\n        frameCountRef.current += 1;\n        if (time - fpsTimerRef.current >= 500) {\n          const currentFps = Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / (time - fpsTimerRef.current));\n          onFpsUpdate(Math.min(currentFps, 120));\n          frameCountRef.current = 0;\n          fpsTimerRef.current = time;\n        }\n\n        const width = container.clientWidth;\n        const height = container.clientHeight;\n        const currentTheme = COLOR_THEMES[settings.colorTheme];\n\n        // トレイル描画（半透明黒で重ね塗り）\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';\n        ctx.fillStyle = currentTheme.background;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0.04, Math.min(0.8, settings.trailLength));\n        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n        // 加算合成で光彩感を演出\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n\n        // ポインタ座標の決定（非操作時はリサージュ曲線で滑らかに自動遊泳）\n        let targetX = pointerRef.current.x;\n        let targetY = pointerRef.current.y;\n\n        if (!pointerRef.current.isActive) {\n          autoFloatTimeRef.current += delta * 0.7 * settings.speed;\n          const t = autoFloatTimeRef.current;\n          targetX = width / 2 + Math.sin(t * 1.3) * (width * 0.35);\n          targetY = height / 2 + Math.cos(t * 0.9) * (height * 0.28);\n        }\n\n        const particles = particlesRef.current;\n        const pCount = particles.length;\n\n        // 物理計算 & 更新\n        for (let i = 0; i < pCount; i++) {\n          const p = particles[i];\n          const dx = targetX - p.x;\n          const dy = targetY - p.y;\n          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n\n          // モード別挙動\n          if (settings.behaviorMode === 'follow') {\n            // 吸い寄せ\n            const force = Math.min(dist * 0.00045 * settings.speed, 0.45);\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * force;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * force;\n          } else if (settings.behaviorMode === 'repel') {\n            // 反発\n            if (dist < 260) {\n              const force = ((260 - dist) / 260) * 0.75 * settings.speed;\n              p.vx += (dx / dist) * force;\n              p.vy += (dy / dist) * force;\n            }\n          } else if (settings.behaviorMode === 'orbit') {\n            // 周回軌道\n            p.angle += p.orbitSpeed * settings.speed;\n            const targetOrbitX = targetX + Math.cos(p.angle) * p.orbitRadius;\n            const targetOrbitY = targetY + Math.sin(p.angle) * p.orbitRadius;\n            p.vx += (targetOrbitX - p.x) * 0.02 * settings.speed;\n            p.vy += (targetOrbitY - p.y) * 0.02 * settings.speed;\n          } else if (settings.behaviorMode === 'stream') {\n            // 流星群（斜め方向の気流＋ポインタ付近での加速）\n            p.vx += 0.08 * settings.speed;\n            p.vy += 0.12 * settings.speed;\n            if (dist < 200) {\n              p.vx += (dx / dist) * 0.3 * settings.speed;\n              p.vy += (dy / dist) * 0.3 * settings.speed;\n            }\n          }\n\n          // クリック中は引力を強化\n          if (pointerRef.current.isDown) {\n            const pressForce = Math.min(dist * 0.001 * settings.speed, 0.8);\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * pressForce;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * pressForce;\n          }\n\n          // 減速摩擦\n          p.vx *= 0.94;\n          p.vy *= 0.94;\n\n          // 速度上限\n          const currentSpeed = Math.hypot(p.vx, p.vy);\n          const maxSpd = 16 * settings.speed;\n          if (currentSpeed > maxSpd) {\n            p.vx = (p.vx / currentSpeed) * maxSpd;\n            p.vy = (p.vy / currentSpeed) * maxSpd;\n          }\n\n          // 座標更新\n          p.x += p.vx;\n          p.y += p.vy;\n\n          // 画面端のラップアラウンド（飛び出し防止）\n          if (p.x < -30) p.x = width + 30;\n          if + 1; j < maxJ; j++) {\n              const p2 = particles[j];\n              const dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n              const dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n              const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n\n 2;\n          b.alpha *= 0.92;\n\n          ctx.save();\n          ctx.beginPath();\n          ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n          ctx.strokeStyle = b.color;\n          ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, (1 - b.radius / b.maxRadius) * 6);\n          ctx.globalAlpha = b.alpha;\n          ctx.stroke();\n          ctx.restore();\n\n          if (b.alpha < 0.03 || b.radius >= b.maxRadius * 0.98) {\n            bursts.splice(i, 1);\n          }\n        }\n\n        // ポインタ中心の微細な発光体\n        if (pointerRef.current.isActive) {\n          ctx.beginPath();\n
